## Separately installed runtime components

Docker Engine/Desktop, NVIDIA drivers/Container Toolkit, WSL, downloaded container
images, and Docker Model Runner inference engines are installed or downloaded
separately and retain their own licenses. They are not included in the connector
Go executable or relicensed by its MIT license. The pinned Docker firewall helper
image includes components under their upstream licenses; its source and image
metadata are available from https://github.com/docker-library/docker.

The connector downloads Dropbear payloads for SSH injection. Their distribution
must retain the Dropbear component notices available in `LICENSE` at
https://github.com/mkj/dropbear and the source/build provenance of the distributed
payload. Windows uses the userspace tunnel with the OS TUN device disabled; no
Wintun DLL is bundled in these release binaries.
